Title: Voigt-profile component table for LMC sightlines
Authors: Suraj Poudel et al.
Table: Component properties from Voigt fits with LMC disk LSR boundaries
================================================================================
Description:
Each row corresponds to one fitted absorption
component from one ion in one sightline. The columns Left_LSR and
Right_LSR are the lmc disk boundaries and unique for each sightline
================================================================================
Byte-by-byte Description of file: lmc_sightlines_apj_mrt.txt
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
   Bytes Format Units  Label      Explanations
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1-18  A18    ---    Sightline  Sightline identifier
 20-23  A4     ---    Ion        Ion species
 25-34  F10.3  km/s   Left_LSR   Left disk boundary in LSR
 36-45  F10.3  km/s   Right_LSR  Right disk boundary in LSR
 47-54  F8.1   km/s   vLSR       Component velocity in the LSR frame
 56-62  F7.1   km/s   e_vLSR     Uncertainty in vLSR
 64-72  F9.1   km/s   vLMCSR     Component velocity in the LMCSR frame
 74-80  F7.3   dex    logN       Log column density
 82-88  F7.3   dex    e_logN     Uncertainty in log column density
 90-96  F7.2   km/s   b          Doppler parameter
 98-104 F7.2   km/s   e_b        Uncertainty in Doppler parameter
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Notes:
1. Velocities are in km/s and logN values are in dex.
================================================================================
